The Mac workflow for the durable-acceptance source fails both its main runtime suite and its settings-without-Metal repeat with an unreadable activation-history error. Its Xcode build passes, as do the rendered memory controls. This failed receipt is retained and does not certify native acceptance. It arrived after [[sources/runs/2026/10/2026-10-04-reviewed-model-setup-offers-preparation]] captured the workflow as running.

Source comparison identifies an outdated corrupt-history fixture: configure now opens the accepted-version journal before selecting a pack, so its existing unchecked configure call throws on the deliberately malformed record. The fixture now requires that earlier refusal and verifies that a subsequent turn remains blocked. It still preserves the corrupt bytes, requires an explicit archive/repair operation and never bypasses model verification. Native confirmation of this diagnosis remains pending the corrected CI run.

The same earlier refusal exposes a product recovery boundary: the outer owner previously refused repair whenever settings were pending, including when applying those settings had failed on the corrupt journal. Explicit repair now remains available for a failed pending configuration with a recoverable owned journal. It passes the latest saved preferences into the concrete inference owner, validates that exact selection before archival, and leaves queued work behind normal pending-configuration and complete-model verification gates. The original no-argument recovery API is retained. Successful repair still preserves every prior record byte, creates no model health receipt and starts no unrequested model.

The runtime fixture changes the live-memory preference after a corrupt-history refusal, requires failed pending settings and recovery availability to coexist, repairs explicitly, then verifies that the queued request reaches missing-model verification under the latest saved live policy. The presentation state and repair button now admit that failure case; a corresponding light/dark/system native rendering fixture is prepared. CI now retains the existing memory-control screenshots with its other UI artifacts for direct review. A bounded milestone line distinguishes accepted-version tests from later recovery fixtures in future failure logs. No local native build, tensor scan or model test ran during the held-out study. Current code execution, visual review and physical recovery acceptance remain pending; no registry entry, profile or installed artifact changed.
